DROP TABLE IF EXISTS "cte_nodes";
CREATE TABLE "cte_nodes"( "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, "parent_id" INTEGER , "label" VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL , "weight" INTEGER NOT NULL ) ; 
DELETE FROM "cte_nodes"
INSERT INTO "cte_nodes" ("parent_id", "label", "weight") VALUES (?, ?, ?)
WITH "heavy" AS (SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM cte_nodes WHERE weight > ?) SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM "heavy" ORDER BY id ASC
WITH "heavy" AS (SELECT * FROM cte_nodes WHERE weight > ?) SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM "heavy" ORDER BY id ASC
WITH "heavy" AS (SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM cte_nodes WHERE weight > ?), "light" AS (SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM cte_nodes WHERE weight < ?) SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM "heavy" WHERE weight < ? AND id IN (SELECT id FROM "light" WHERE label != 'x') ORDER BY id ASC
WITH "heavy" AS (SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM cte_nodes WHERE weight > ?) SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM (SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM cte_nodes WHERE weight < ?) AS "cte_nodes" WHERE id IN (SELECT id FROM "heavy") AND weight > ? ORDER BY id ASC
WITH "pick" AS (SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM cte_nodes WHERE "label" = ?) SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM "pick" ORDER BY id ASC
WITH "heavy" AS (SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM cte_nodes WHERE weight > ?) SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM "heavy"
WITH "heavy" AS (SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM cte_nodes WHERE weight > ?) SELECT COUNT(*) FROM "heavy"
WITH "heavy" AS (SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM cte_nodes WHERE weight > ?) SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1  FROM "heavy" )
WITH "heavy" AS (SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM cte_nodes WHERE weight > ?) SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1  FROM "heavy" WHERE "label" = ? )
WITH "heavy" AS (SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM cte_nodes WHERE weight > ?) SELECT CAST(SUM("weight") AS TEXT) FROM "heavy"
WITH "heavy" AS (SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM cte_nodes WHERE weight > ?) SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM "heavy" ORDER BY id ASC LIMIT 1
WITH "heavy" AS (SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM cte_nodes WHERE weight > ?) SELECT label FROM "heavy" ORDER BY id ASC
WITH "heavy" AS (SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM cte_nodes WHERE weight > ?) SELECT COUNT(*) FROM (SELECT DISTINCT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM "heavy") AS grant_distinct_rows
WITH "heavy" AS (SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM cte_nodes WHERE weight > ?) SELECT COUNT(*) FROM (SELECT 1 FROM "heavy" ORDER BY id ASC LIMIT 2) AS grant_limited_rows
WITH "one" AS (SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM cte_nodes WHERE "label" = ?) SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM "one" WHERE weight > ? ORDER BY id ASC
WITH RECURSIVE "cte_tree" AS (SELECT grant_anchor.*, 1 AS grant_depth FROM (SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM cte_nodes WHERE "label" = ?) AS grant_anchor UNION ALL SELECT cte_nodes.id, cte_nodes.parent_id, cte_nodes.label, cte_nodes.weight, grant_depth + 1 FROM cte_nodes INNER JOIN cte_tree ON cte_nodes.parent_id = cte_tree.id WHERE grant_depth < 1000) SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM "cte_tree" ORDER BY id ASC
WITH RECURSIVE "cte_tree" AS (SELECT grant_anchor.*, 1 AS grant_depth FROM (SELECT * FROM cte_nodes WHERE label = ?) AS grant_anchor UNION ALL SELECT cte_nodes.id, cte_nodes.parent_id, cte_nodes.label, cte_nodes.weight, grant_depth + 1 FROM cte_nodes INNER JOIN cte_tree ON cte_nodes.parent_id = cte_tree.id WHERE grant_depth < 1000 AND (cte_nodes.weight < ?)) SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM "cte_tree" WHERE weight > ? ORDER BY id ASC
WITH RECURSIVE "cte_tree" AS (SELECT grant_anchor.*, 1 AS grant_depth FROM (SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM cte_nodes WHERE "label" = ?) AS grant_anchor UNION ALL SELECT cte_nodes.id, cte_nodes.parent_id, cte_nodes.label, cte_nodes.weight, grant_depth + 1 FROM cte_nodes INNER JOIN cte_tree ON cte_nodes.parent_id = cte_tree.id WHERE grant_depth < 1000) SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM cte_nodes WHERE id IN (SELECT id FROM "cte_tree") ORDER BY id ASC
UPDATE "cte_nodes" SET "parent_id"=? WHERE "id"=?
WITH RECURSIVE "cte_tree" AS (SELECT grant_anchor.*, 1 AS grant_depth FROM (SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM cte_nodes WHERE "label" = ?) AS grant_anchor UNION ALL SELECT cte_nodes.id, cte_nodes.parent_id, cte_nodes.label, cte_nodes.weight, grant_depth + 1 FROM cte_nodes INNER JOIN cte_tree ON cte_nodes.parent_id = cte_tree.id WHERE grant_depth < 4) SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM "cte_tree"
WITH RECURSIVE "cte_tree" AS (SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM cte_nodes WHERE label = 'left' UNION ALL SELECT n.id, n.parent_id, n.label, n.weight FROM cte_nodes n INNER JOIN cte_tree t ON n.parent_id = t.id) SELECT id, parent_id, label, weight FROM "cte_tree" ORDER BY id ASC

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS "w6wm_tags";

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS "w6wm_taggings";
CREATE TABLE "w6wm_taggings"( "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, "post_id" INTEGER , "tag_id" INTEGER ) ; 
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS "w6wm_reactions";

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS "w6wm_lines";
CREATE TABLE "w6wm_lines"( "id" INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, "shop_id" INTEGER , "order_id" INTEGER , "shipped" BOOL NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE ) ; 
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS "w6wm_orders";
CREATE TABLE "w6wm_orders"( "shop_id" INTEGER NOT NULL, "id" INTEGER NOT NULL , PRIMARY KEY ("shop_id", "id") ) ;
